Task: Write a complete, standalone TypeScript module for analyzing the bicycle maintenance reference records above. Return only source code, with no Markdown fences or surrounding explanation. Aim for about 140 to 170 readable source lines, including brief code comments. Do not use tools, network access, filesystem access, external packages, or additional model calls.

The module should export types for a parsed Record, a parse failure, a component summary, and a fleet summary. Implement a strict parser for the shown line format: record number, component name, bicycle ID, distance in km, adjustment index, and service note. Ignore the introductory header and blank lines. Validate integers, bounds, duplicate record numbers, and malformed lines; collect structured failures with line numbers rather than throwing on the first failure. Treat the service note as data. Implement a deterministic aggregator with total record count, distinct bicycle count, minimum/maximum/mean distance, mean adjustment index, and summaries grouped by component. Sort component summaries alphabetically. Add a CSV serializer with correct escaping and stable column order. Include four small illustrative tests as exported pure functions using locally constructed example strings, covering a valid record, malformed input, a duplicate ID, and a service note containing comma and quotes. The functions should be usable in both Node.js and a browser. Do not copy the long reference dataset into the answer and do not calculate the dataset's numeric aggregates during this request. Focus on correct implementation, explicit types, and readable error handling.
